([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-t-male-formulation-against) · ★12)
- **endo-t-measurement-diagnosis-against**: The guideline recommends against routinely measuring testosterone in women for diagnostic purposes due to the lack of a reliable correlation between symptoms and testosterone levels. This recommendation is triggered when a clinician orders a testosterone test to assess fatigue, low libido, or mood symptoms without evidence of underlying endocrine pathology. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-t-measurement-diagnosis-against) · ★12)
- **endo-t-monitoring-early-and-6mo**: This skill recommends monitoring serum testosterone levels 3–6 weeks after initiating therapy and every 6 months thereafter to detect overuse or signs of androgen excess. It is triggered when a clinician manages a woman on testosterone therapy requiring early safety assessment and ongoing surveillance for adverse effects. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-t-monitoring-early-and-6mo) · ★12)
- **endo-t-ongoing-monitoring-6mo**: Recommends reviewing serum testosterone levels every 6 months during ongoing therapy to monitor for excessive use and signs of androgen excess such as hirsutism or acne. It is indicated when a clinician manages a woman receiving long-term testosterone therapy and needs to evaluate for adverse effects or consider dose adjustment. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-t-ongoing-monitoring-6mo) · ★12)
- **endo-t-trial-baseline-nonoral**: Recommends checking baseline testosterone level and using an approved non-oral preparation (such as transdermal patch, gel, or cream) when initiating a testosterone trial for hypoactive sexual desire disorder (HSDD) in women. Triggered when a clinician plans to start a T trial for HSDD and needs to confirm baseline levels and select an appropriate delivery method. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-t-trial-baseline-nonoral) · ★12)
- **endo-target-age-normalized-igf1**: Suggests a biochemical treatment goal of age-normalized serum IGF-1 value to indicate disease control in acromegaly. Use when setting therapeutic targets after initiating medical therapy or surgery, e.g., post-op IGF-1 at 12 weeks or random GH &gt;1 µg/L. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-target-age-normalized-igf1) · ★12)
- **endo-testosterone-therapy-symptomatic-low-testosterone**: Suggests testosterone therapy as symptomatically indicated for low testosterone levels, and not for improving dyslipidemia or cardiovascular disease risk. Trigger phrases include "Patient with low testosterone symptomatic", "Considering testosterone for hypogonadism symptoms", and "Assessing appropriate indications for testosterone replacement". ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-testosterone-therapy-symptomatic-low-testosterone) · ★12)
- **endo-tg-check-bile-acid-sequestrant**: This skill recommends checking triglyceride levels before and after starting a bile acid sequestrant in patients with elevated triglycerides (150-499 mg/dL). Clinical triggers include: triglyceride level 150-499 mg/dL, consideration of bile acid sequestrant for hypertriglyceridemia, and monitoring of therapeutic response to bile acid sequestrant. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/lipidology/endo-tg-check-bile-acid-sequestrant) · ★12)
- **endo-therapeutic-goal-random-gh-1**: Suggests using a random GH level &lt;1.0 µg/L as a therapeutic goal to assess biochemical control of acromegaly during follow‑up after surgery or medical therapy. Use when evaluating post‑treatment response, particularly when random GH is measured alongside IGF‑1 to determine if disease is controlled. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-therapeutic-goal-random-gh-1) · ★12)
- **endo-thyroid-us-palpable-nodule**: Suggests thyroid ultrasound when a palpable thyroid nodule is detected on physical examination in patients with acromegaly. Trigger phrases include "palpable thyroid nodule," "thyroid nodularity on palpation," or "feeling a thyroid lump." ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/thyroid-nodule/endo-thyroid-us-palpable-nodule) · ★12)
- **endo-trial-cabergoline-modest-igf1-mild**: Suggests a trial of a dopamine agonist, usually cabergoline, as initial adjuvant therapy in patients with only modest IGF-1 elevations and mild GH excess symptoms after surgery. Use when postoperative IGF-1 is mildly elevated and symptoms of GH excess are mild. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-trial-cabergoline-modest-igf1-mild) · ★12)
- **endo-us-gallstones-if-symptomatic**: Recommend performing abdominal ultrasound when a patient on somatostatin receptor ligand (SRL) therapy develops signs or symptoms suggestive of gallstone disease. Trigger phrases include biliary colic, postprandial right upper quadrant pain, Murphy's sign, or suspected cholecystitis. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-us-gallstones-if-symptomatic) · ★12)
- **endo-use-shortacting-octreotide-preconception**: Recommends utilizing short-acting octreotide subcutaneous injections for disease control while awaiting conception after discontinuing long-acting somatostatin receptor ligands or pegvisomant. Trigger phrases include “preconception window,” “discontinuing long-acting SRL,” and “short-acting octreotide as needed until conception.” ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-use-shortacting-octreotide-preconception) · ★12)
- **endo-visual-field-optic-chiasm**: Suggests performing formal visual field testing when imaging reveals tumor abutting the optic chiasm to evaluate for visual pathway compression. Trigger phrases include MRI or CT showing tumor-chiasm contact. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/endo-visual-field-optic-chiasm) · ★12)
- **endo-vit-d-supp-children-adolescents**: Suggests empiric vitamin D supplementation to prevent nutritional rickets and potentially lower respiratory tract infection risk in children and adolescents aged 1 to 18 years. Use when a clinician asks about vitamin D supplementation for patients aged 1 to 18 years to prevent rickets or respiratory infections. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vit-d-supp-children-adolescents) · ★12)
- **endo-vit-d-supp-high-risk-prediabetes**: Suggests empiric vitamin D supplementation plus lifestyle modification to reduce diabetes progression risk in adults with high-risk prediabetes. Use when a clinician considers vitamin D for patients with prediabetes meeting 2 or more ADA glycemic criteria (fasting glucose, HbA1c, 2‑hour OGTT) to prevent diabetes. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vit-d-supp-high-risk-prediabetes) · ★12)
- **endo-vit-d-supp-over-75**: The skill suggests empiric vitamin D supplementation to lower mortality risk in adults aged 75 years and older. Use when a clinician inquires about vitamin D supplementation for patients aged 75 years or older to reduce mortality risk. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vit-d-supp-over-75) · ★12)
- **endo-vit-d-supp-pregnancy**: The guideline suggests empiric vitamin D supplementation during pregnancy to lower the risk of preeclampsia, intra-uterine mortality, preterm birth, small-for-gestational-age birth, and neonatal mortality. Consider this when a clinician inquires about vitamin D supplementation for pregnant patients to reduce pregnancy complications. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vit-d-supp-pregnancy) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-daily-vs-intermittent-dosing**: Recommends daily lower-dose vitamin D over intermittent high-dose dosing for adults aged 50 years and older who have indications for vitamin D supplementation. Trigger phrases include: "Should I prescribe daily or intermittent vitamin D for this patient?" and "What dosing frequency is best for vitamin D in adults over 50? ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-daily-vs-intermittent-dosing) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-screening-dark-complexion**: The guideline recommends against routine 25(OH)D screening and subsequent vitamin D treatment in adults with dark complexion who lack established indications for testing. Consider this recommendation when clinicians ask, "Should I screen for vitamin D deficiency in this patient with dark skin?" or "Is vitamin D testing indicated based on skin complexion?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-screening-dark-complexion) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-screening-healthy**: Recommends against routine 25(OH)D screening and subsequent vitamin D treatment in healthy adults without established indications. Triggered by clinician questions such as "Should I screen for vitamin D deficiency in this healthy adult?" or "Is vitamin D testing indicated for asymptomatic patients?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-screening-healthy) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-screening-obesity**: This skill recommends against routine 25-hydroxyvitamin D [25(OH)D] screening and subsequent vitamin D supplementation in adults with obesity when no established indication exists. It is triggered by clinician questions such as “Should I screen for vitamin D deficiency in this obese patient?” or “Is vitamin D testing indicated based on BMI?” ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-screening-obesity) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-supplementation-50to74**: Recommends against routine vitamin D supplementation beyond the Dietary Reference Intake in adults aged 50-74 years without established indications for vitamin D treatment. Triggers include clinician questions such as "Should I prescribe vitamin D to this middle-aged adult?" or "Is vitamin D supplementation needed for patients aged 50-74?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-supplementation-50to74) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-supplementation-under50**: Recommends against empiric vitamin D supplementation beyond the Dietary Reference Intake in adults younger than 50 years. Triggered by questions such as "Should I prescribe vitamin D to this young adult?" or "Is vitamin D supplementation needed for patients under 50?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-supplementation-under50) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-testing-50to74**: This skill recommends against routine 25(OH)D testing in asymptomatic adults aged 50–74 years. It is triggered by questions such as "Should I order a vitamin D level for this patient aged 50-74?" or "Is vitamin D testing indicated for asymptomatic middle-aged adults?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-testing-50to74) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-testing-over75**: Recommends against routine 25(OH)D testing in adults aged 75 years and older. Triggered by questions such as "Should I order a vitamin D level for this patient over 75?" or "Is vitamin D testing indicated for asymptomatic elderly adults?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-testing-over75) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-testing-pregnancy**: Recommends against routine 25(OH)D testing during pregnancy. Triggers include: "Should I order a vitamin D level for this pregnant patient?" and "Is vitamin D testing indicated for asymptomatic pregnant patients? ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-testing-pregnancy) · ★12)
- **endo-vitd-no-testing-under50**: Recommends against routine 25(OH)D testing in adults younger than 50 years. Triggers include clinician questions such as "Should I order a vitamin D level for this patient under 50?" or "Is vitamin D testing indicated for asymptomatic young adults?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-no-testing-under50) · ★12)

- **endo-vitd-supplementation-prediabetes**: Suggests empiric vitamin D supplementation plus lifestyle modification to reduce diabetes progression in adults with high-risk prediabetes. Triggered by clinician questions such as "Should I prescribe vitamin D to this patient with prediabetes?" or "Is vitamin D supplementation indicated for prediabetes management?" ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/preventive-care/endo-vitd-supplementation-prediabetes) · ★12)

- **endo-weightloss-maint**: This skill suggests using approved weight‑loss medication over no pharmacological therapy to ameliorate comorbidities and improve physical activity in adults with BMI ≥30 kg/m² or BMI ≥27 kg/m² with at least one comorbid condition. It is triggered when a clinician asks, for example, 'Should I add medication to help maintain weight loss in this patient with BMI 31 and diabetes?' or 'Is pharmacotherapy appropriate for long‑term weight control in a patient with BMI 28 and hypertension?'. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/obesity/endo-weightloss-maint) · ★12)
- **endo-weightneutral-t2dm**: Recommends weight‑losing and weight‑neutral medications as first‑ and second‑line agents for managing overweight/obese patients with type 2 diabetes. Triggers include when a clinician asks, 'Which diabetes medications will not worsen weight in this obese patient?' or 'Should I avoid sulfonylureas in this patient with T2DM and obesity due to weight gain risk?' ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/obesity/endo-weightneutral-t2dm) · ★12)
- **endo-withhold-medical-therapy-pregnancy-except-tumor-headache**: Recommends that medical therapy for acromegaly be withheld during pregnancy and administered only for tumor control and headache relief. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/endo-withhold-medical-therapy-pregnancy-except-tumor-headache) · ★12)

- **endorser**: Use when modelling one endorser — the singular model of the endorsers collection (the plural store); a party who signs over or backs an instrument. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/vocabulary/endorser) · ★2)
- **endorsers**: Use when reasoning about endorsers — People or organizations that endorse the plan. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/vocabulary/endorsers) · ★2)
- **endowment-effect**: Las personas valoran más lo que poseen. Use cuando diseñe trials, personalizaciones, colecciones, o cualquier sistema donde usuarios "posean" algo digital. - **endpaper**: Riso-grain flat-vector collection of chill characters or objects scattered across a fully saturated solid color field — like the inside endpaper of a children's picture book. Two-tone interior shading on every character, closed crescent-eye sleepy smiles, soft hand-drawn line details on props, dense overlapping composition where elements touch the canvas edges. Trigger when the user says /endpaper, asks for an "endpaper illustration", a "scattered animal or object collection on saturated color", a "children's-book endpaper scene", or briefs with a cast + background hue + density + palette. ([vm0-ai/vm0-skills](https://github.com/vm0-ai/vm0-skills/tree/HEAD/illustration-template/endpaper) · ★76)
- **endpoint**: Use when creating or editing a FastAPI endpoint under src/presentation/http/v1/endpoints/&lt;audience&gt;/. Enforces audience-first organization (public/user/admin/internal), the exact parameter-naming convention (data for bodies, query for GET filters), the five-parameter list shape, correct Require + Authorization wiring, and strict contract ↔ Result mapping. ([ocbunknown/fastapi-claude-template](https://github.com/ocbunknown/fastapi-claude-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/endpoint) · ★32)
- **endpoint**: Use when modelling one endpoint — the singular model of the endpoints collection (the plural store); an addressable interface where a service is reached. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/vocabulary/endpoint) · ★2)
